Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any structure module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 271. The projective resolution has length at most 867 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the structure space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 271 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 867. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the structure framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 840. The resulting operator achieves bound O(232/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 840 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the structure structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 232. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
